- Baseline change · Recurrent systemic flares and reduced speech tolerance · Tingling and green diarrhea recurred during overlapping gou teng, SuperHMO and other exposures. He worried about a new fatigue phenotype. July 27 he could communicate using brief yes/no questions but had difficulty speaking at length and tracking conversation. July 31 a violent crash followed both recent activity and xylitol intake. · prior communication and GI function · His prediction of a future baseline loss on July 20 is not evidence that a permanent loss occurred. Hearing protection and cognitive load also affected conversation. · baseline_states:BL-19
- Clinical episode · Gou teng, SuperHMO, selegiline withdrawal, telmisartan changes and garlic cessation overlap with a reported new corrosive/poisoned pattern, diarrhea/green stools, and two similar consecutive days with fatigue, sleep pressure and aggressive PEM. · July deterioration with overlapping exposures · clinical_episodes:S2-P-645

- Correspondence · 2025-07-15T14:58:25+02:00 · I would have agreed with it more strongly pre-pandemic I feel that ME/CFS is a more of genetic disease triggered by pathogens than a pathogenic disease triggered by genetics - gene variants in mtDNA or peroxisomal function, variants which cause immunosuppression or impair autophagy, etc are required moreso than pathogens themselves For example, almost everyone has EBV or has had SARS-CoV-2 To compare, very few people get ALS after a pathogen (say, SARS-CoV-2, Babesia) - ALS has a stronger genetic component than ME, however I would still argue that ME has a relatively strong genetic component · researcher commentary · 2025-07-15T14:58:25+02:00 · archive_messages:S1-M46551
